# THE EXAM DOCKET — Numbers 28:1-29:39 (the offerings calendar: the continual offering, the Sabbath, the new moon, the festivals' additional offerings), THE NUMBERS WALK sitting 9b, the compile (2026-09-11; the owner: "Ok go" on 1b's order — the measurements, the design, the parser, the docket, the types, the runner; the design World/step9/NUMBERS_WALK.md "Sitting 9b").
# DECLARED: the exam docket is the UNION (the standing rule of 2026-09-05) of (1) THE LINK-DRIVEN ROWS — every segment of the local shelf's Babylonian Talmud, Mishnah and Tosefta exports whose English cites a verse of Numbers 28:1-31 or 29:1-39 (the scan by script over Data/sefaria_export, commentaries excluded: 135 rows in 26 works — Menachot 27, Yoma 16, Pesachim 11, Zevachim 10, Megillah 9, Rosh Hashanah 9, Taanit 9, Shabbat 7, Sukkah 7, Arakhin 4, Shevuot 4, Temurah 4, Meilah 3, Mishnah Megillah 2, Tamid 2, Bava Kamma 1, Beitzah 1, Bekhorot 1, Chullin 1, Horayot 1, Mishnah Menachot 1, Mishnah Shevuot 1, Mishnah Ta anit 1, Mishnah Zevachim 1, Nedarim 1, Tosefta Megillah Lieberman 1), and (2) THE TOPIC-ROUTED ROWS — the rows the three reading ledgers' TESTING SHELF lines (num_28_daily_shabbat_rosh, num_28_pesach_shavuot, num_29_fall_festivals, all 2026-09-11) and COMPILE_DEBT's sitting-9 box name BY ADDRESS: the Mishnah rows Mishnah Tamid 4:1, Mishnah Menachot 4:2, Mishnah Menachot 4:3, Mishnah Menachot 4:4, Mishnah Menachot 6:6, Mishnah Menachot 6:7, Mishnah Menachot 8:4, Mishnah Menachot 8:5, Mishnah Menachot 9:2, Mishnah Menachot 9:3, Mishnah Menachot 9:4, Mishnah Menachot 9:5, Mishnah Menachot 10:6, Mishnah Shekalim 4:1, Mishnah Temurah 2:1, Mishnah Pesachim 2:5, Mishnah Sukkah 4:9, Mishnah Beitzah 2:4, Mishnah Shevuot 1:4, Mishnah Shevuot 1:5 (20 rows — Mishnah Menachot 8:7, Shevuot 1:3, Zevachim 10:1 and Megillah 3:5-6 entered through the link scan; the topic list's "Mishnah_Taanit" key missed the export's spelling "Mishnah_Ta_anit", and Mishnah Taanit 4:2 — the watches' institution on 28:2 — entered as the link row "Mishnah Ta anit 4:2"; Mishnah Beitzah 2:4 is the mishna printed at Beitzah 19a:11, read there), and THIRTY-SIX FOLIO RANGES READ WHOLE BY ADDRESS — every segment of each range on the export, the link rows among them read once: Yoma 62b-63a (38 segments, 2 link); Yoma 46a-46a (14 segments, 1 link); Yoma 85b-85b (14 segments, 0 link); Menachot 44b-45b (61 segments, 5 link); Menachot 49a-50a (57 segments, 1 link); Menachot 65b-66a (32 segments, 0 link); Menachot 68b-68b (26 segments, 1 link); Menachot 84b-84b (21 segments, 2 link); Menachot 87a-88a (69 segments, 5 link); Menachot 89a-89a (20 segments, 1 link); Menachot 110a-110a (23 segments, 0 link); Pesachim 35a-35a (15 segments, 0 link); Pesachim 58a-59a (46 segments, 3 link); Pesachim 66a-66a (13 segments, 2 link); Pesachim 77a-77a (19 segments, 1 link); Pesachim 80b-81a (34 segments, 0 link); Pesachim 95b-95b (18 segments, 0 link); Shabbat 103b-103b (17 segments, 1 link); Shabbat 132b-132b (16 segments, 0 link); Taanit 2b-3a (31 segments, 7 link); Sukkah 34a-34a (14 segments, 0 link); Sukkah 44a-44a (11 segments, 0 link); Sukkah 47a-51a (93 segments, 6 link); Sukkah 55b-55b (16 segments, 0 link); Zevachim 110b-110b (9 segments, 1 link); Rosh Hashanah 4b-5a (25 segments, 1 link); Rosh Hashanah 16a-16a (17 segments, 0 link); Rosh Hashanah 32a-34a (96 segments, 4 link); Shevuot 2a-2b (20 segments, 1 link); Shevuot 9a-13a (121 segments, 3 link); Beitzah 12a-12a (11 segments, 0 link); Beitzah 19a-20b (60 segments, 0 link); Chagigah 6a-6b (32 segments, 0 link); Chagigah 17a-18a (32 segments, 0 link); Berakhot 26a-26a (21 segments, 0 link); Arakhin 13a-13a (11 segments, 0 link): 1145 rows, link-duplicates dropped. 191 addresses (29 link, 162 topic) were ALREADY VERDICTED in earlier ledgers (the Exodus triage ledger, the incense-shekel docket, the Egypt block, the dependency-debt docket, the erection docket the largest shares) and are marked CREDITED with the ledger named — each given its QUICK LOOK here (speed ruling (b), credit guard (1)), never a blind credit. Every row carries ONE verdict: LAW (a rule the compiled function answers — the CELL named F1..F7 per the design, or the callee's cell by CALL), DERIVATION (a hook from the verse to a rule), DISPUTE (a parameter row), CONTEXT (the page's matter outside the span's rules, the prayer's, another engine's, or an empty export segment), OUTSIDE (none needed: every range was read whole). The Sifrei on Numbers piskaot 142-151 (the reading shelf) were read at sitting 9, not here.
# THE TALMUD ADDRESSING: the export's index 0 is folio 1a (a page = index // 2 + 1; measured 2026-09-09); the Mishnah by chapter:mishnah; the Tosefta by the export's chapter:segment (Lieberman).

## Arakhin
- Arakhin 3b:15 [LINK — Num 29:1] — DERIVATION. 29:1 'a day of sounding FOR YOU' — everyone obligated in the shofar, priests included (the trumpets' 10:10 not exempting them); the shofar's law the Rosh Hashanah tractate's — F5 day_of_blowing carries 'for you' = the obligation on all.
- Arakhin 10b:1 [LINK — Num 28:24 Num 29:12 Num 29:13 Num 29:14 Num 29:15 Num 29:16 Num 29:17 Num 29:18 Num 29:19 Num 29:20 Num 29:21 Num 29:22 Num 29:23 Num 29:24 Num 29:25 Num 29:26 Num 29:27 Num 29:28 Num 29:29 Num 29:30 Num 29:31 Num 29:32 Num 29:33 Num 29:34 Num 29:35 Num 29:36 Num 29:37 Num 29:38] — LAW. the full hallel every day of Sukkot because its DAYS ARE DISTINCT by their musafim (the bulls decline, 29:12-38) and only the first day of Pesach because its days are alike (28:24 'as these') — the tables' structure read as law: F6 the_declining_bulls against F4 as_these.
- Arakhin 13b:2 [LINK — Num 28:2 — CREDITED: EXOD_TALMUD_TRIAGE_LEDGER.md, exodus_block_pesach_2026-09-04.md] — LAW. CREDITED (the Exodus triage, the Pesach block) — the tamid inspected FOUR DAYS before slaughter by the identity 'keep' (28:2 tishmeru) / 'a keeping' (Exod 12:6 mishmeret): the Sifrei 142:2's identity — F1 keep (I2).
- Arakhin 18b:7 [LINK — Num 28:3 — CREDITED: EXOD_TALMUD_TRIAGE_LEDGER.md] — DERIVATION. CREDITED (the Exodus triage) — 'sheep in their first year' (28:3): the year of an offering's age is a whole year from the day, not the calendar's — the Shelach runner's row lamb_and_ram_ages READ by CALL.

## Bava Kamma
- Bava Kamma 111a:12 [LINK — Num 28:23] — LAW. 'besides the burnt offering of the morning' (28:23) — the inference the Sage puts to Rava: the musafim after the morning tamid (the order, not before) — F4 the stack / F2 between_the_temidim.

## Beitzah
- Beitzah 15b:16 [LINK — Num 29:35] — DISPUTE. 'an assembly FOR YOU' (29:35) against Deut 16:8's 'for the LORD your God' — R. Eliezer: all for God or all for you; R. Yehoshua: half and half — the parameter atzeret_for_you on F6 the_eighth.

## Bekhorot
- Bekhorot 17a:13 [LINK — Num 28:15] — LAW. the new moon's goat 'ONE male goat' (28:15) — the superfluous 'one': a goat proper (the son of a she-goat), R. Meir conceding — F3 the_goat_to_the_lord's species clause.

- Chullin 60b:4 [LINK — Num 28:15 — CREDITED: pre_sinai_docket_2026-09-06.md] — CONTEXT. CREDITED (the pre-Sinai docket) — Resh Lakish's aggadah: 'to the LORD' (28:15) as the moon's atonement 'for Me' — the ink's fact (the one goat 'to the LORD') seated at F3; the reading homiletic.

## Horayot
- Horayot 12b:21 [LINK — Num 28:23] — LAW. Abaye: 'besides the burnt offering of the morning, which is a CONTINUAL burnt offering' (28:23) — the redundant 'continual' teaches THE FREQUENT PRECEDES — F2 between_the_temidim / F5 the_stack's order rule; Mishnah Zevachim 10:1 the answer sheet.

## Megillah
- Megillah 20b:12 [LINK — Num 29:1] — LAW. the shofar by DAY (29:1 'a DAY of sounding'), the musafim by day (Lev 23:37 'each on its day'): the calendar's timers fire on days — F5 day_of_blowing, F7 timers.
- Megillah 21b:17 [LINK — Num 28:1 Num 28:2 Num 28:3 Num 28:4 Num 28:5 Num 28:6 Num 28:7 Num 28:8] — CONTEXT. the new moon's Torah reading divided among four readers — THE THREE PARAGRAPHS 28:1-8, 9-10, 11-15 are the reading's own three registers (the tamid, the Sabbath, the new moon): the ink's paragraphing confirmed from the reading-rule's side.
- Megillah 21b:18 [LINK — Num 28:9 Num 28:11] — CONTEXT. the division continued (two verses for the Sabbath's paragraph, five for the new moon's) — the register sizes: 8 / 2 / 5 verses.
- Megillah 28a:10 [LINK — Num 28:4] — CONTEXT. R. Akiva's question to R. Nechunya — 'lamb' in the singular, why 'ONE'? (28:4) — the question the Sifrei 142:4 answers (not more than one); the story its frame.
- Megillah 29b:3 [LINK — Num 28:14] — LAW. R. Yoshiyah: 'the burnt offering of each new moon IN ITS RENEWAL throughout the months of the year' (28:14) — RENEW the year: the offerings bought from the NEW collection of shekels — F3 in_its_month (the shekel year's start; Mishnah Shekalim 4:1).

## Meilah
- Meilah 11b:12 [LINK — Num 28:7] — LAW. 'IN SANCTITY pour a libation of strong drink' (28:7) — the congealed wine from the altar's drainpipes burned in sanctity: as its pouring, so its burning (R. Elazar b. R. Tzadok, once in seventy years) — F1 strong_drink's 'in the holy place'.
- Meilah 11b:13 [LINK — Num 28:7 — CREDITED: EXOD_TALMUD_TRIAGE_LEDGER.md, incense_shekel_docket_2026-09-06.md] — DERIVATION. CREDITED (the Exodus triage, the incense-shekel docket) — Ravina: the burning by the verbal analogy sanctity (28:7) / sanctity (Exod 29:34 the leftovers burned) — a taught transfer.
- Meilah 13b:8 [LINK — Num 29:31] — LAW. R. Akiva (via R. Elazar): 'and ITS LIBATIONS' plural at 29:31 — TWO libations, the water's and the wine's: the plural of the sixth day's word read for the water libation — F6 the_water_libation, the extra yod's seat (M-28's letter read by ben Beteira; here the PLURAL read by R. Akiva — a fourth form of the derivation, recorded in the parameter's settings).

## Menachot
- Menachot 44b:3 [LINK — Num 29:18] — DISPUTE. 'their meal offering and their libations' (29:18) — the baraita: the animal, then the meal offering, then the libations; Rebbi from Lev 23:37: the animal, the libations, then the meal offering — the parameter order_after_the_animal on F6 the_pointer_line.
- Menachot 44b:13 [LINK — Num 29:18 Num 29:33] — LAW. 'according to the ordinance' (29:18) and 'according to THEIR ordinance' (29:33) — NO DEVIATION from the prescription: the pointer line is binding — F6 the_pointer_line (the mishna's bulls, rams and sheep are not Sukkot's).
- Menachot 45a:5 [LINK — Num 28:11] — CONTEXT. Ezekiel 46:6's new moon (ONE bull, six lambs, a ram) against 28:11's two bulls, a ram, seven lambs — the run's altered table in the Prophets read against the spec: a readback item for the Prophets' walk (the prophet's table the shelf reconciles).
- Menachot 45a:10 [LINK — Num 28:31] — DERIVATION. 'THEY SHALL BE' (28:31) — the offerings brought precisely as prescribed: Shavuot's second set's animals indispensable to one another by 'they shall be' — the availability ladder's counterpart at F4 the_second_set (the ladder 'even one' the Sifrei's; 'they shall be' the Gemara's — both recorded).
- Menachot 45a:11 [LINK — Num 28:11] — CONTEXT. Ezekiel 45:18's 'you shall purify' on the first of Nisan against 28:11's two burnt-offering bulls — R. Yochanan: 'this passage Elijah will interpret' — the shelf's own OPEN verdict on a Prophets seat; filed.
- Menachot 46b:21 [LINK — Num 28:26] — DERIVATION. Abaye: the two loaves are CALLED A MEAL OFFERING — 'when you bring a NEW MEAL OFFERING' (28:26) — hence the priests' scruple about the half-shekels; F4 firstfruits_day names the loaves.
- Menachot 49b:8 [LINK — Num 28:2 — CREDITED: EXOD_TALMUD_TRIAGE_LEDGER.md] — LAW. CREDITED (the Exodus triage) — ben Bag Bag: the tamid's four-day inspection by 'you shall keep' (28:2) / 'a keeping' (Exod 12:6) — F1 keep (I2); Arakhin 13b:2's baraita.
- Menachot 51a:1 [LINK — Num 28:10] — LAW. the tamid overrides Shabbat — 'the burnt offering of every Shabbat, beside the continual burnt offering' (28:10) — and the high priest's griddle-cake, likened to the tamid's meal offering, overrides with it — F2 overrides (IS.tamid('sabbath_overrides') by CALL) with the chavitin as the derived case.
- Menachot 52a:9 [LINK — Num 28:2] — LAW. R. Shimon: 'command... My offering... you shall keep' (28:2) INCLUDES the bull for a communal unwitting sin and the goats of idolatry — their funds from the chamber's collection — F1 keep (from the chamber's fund — the Sifrei 142:2's 'you shall keep' read for the fund).
- Menachot 52a:14 [LINK — Num 29:18] — LAW. 'their meal offering and their libations' (29:18) — the accompaniments may be brought at NIGHT and on the NEXT DAY (Menachot 44b): the libations' timing free of the animal's — F6 the_pointer_line's timing row (a data row: night and the morrow).
- Menachot 65a:9 [LINK — Num 28:4] — LAW. the Sadducees' reading — 'the one lamb YOU SHALL OFFER' (28:4) singular: an individual may donate the tamid — the refused arm recorded at F1 the_one_lamb / keep.
- Menachot 65a:10 [LINK — Num 28:2] — LAW. the Sages' reply — 'you shall KEEP' (28:2) PLURAL: the tamid from the chamber, communal — F1 keep (the communal source by the plural verb; the Sifrei's 'you shall keep' thrice); Megillat Taanit's first days of Nisan the record.
- Menachot 68b:23 [LINK — Num 28:26] — DERIVATION. R. Akiva: the omer from BARLEY because the two loaves must be 'the FIRST FRUITS... a new meal offering' (28:26) — the first of wheat; F4 firstfruits_day with MIN.omer('source') by CALL.
- Menachot 72b:9 [LINK — Num 29:39] — LAW. 'THESE you shall do to the LORD in your appointed times' (29:39) — the rest of the communal offerings override Shabbat and impurity — F7 these_you_shall_do (the calendar's close as the override's source).
- Menachot 84a:13 [LINK — Num 28:26] — DERIVATION. R. Akiva's barley proof from 28:26 again; the baraita refutes R. Yochanan — the omer from the new crop ('the first of your harvest').
- Menachot 84b:2 [LINK — Num 28:26] — LAW. 'a NEW meal offering' (28:26) with Lev 2:11's 'a first offering' — the two loaves the first of all meal offerings from the new crop — F4 firstfruits_day (the Sifrei 148:1's 'the newest of all').
- Menachot 84b:3 [LINK — Num 28:26] — DERIVATION. 'new' twice — 28:26 and Lev 23:16: the first of wheat AND the first of barley (the sotah's meal offering) — the doubled word read for the second grain.
- Menachot 87a:3 [LINK — Num 28:19 Num 28:20] — LAW. R. Yosei b. R. Yehuda: wine with flour-like scum UNFIT — 'unblemished they shall be for you... and their libations' (28:19-20, 31): the libations likened to the beast's wholeness — F4 libations_likened (the Sifrei 149:2's hekkesh).
- Menachot 87a:6 [LINK — Num 28:14] — DISPUTE. wine aged a year — Rebbi unfit (28:14's 'lamb... wine' juxtaposed: as the lamb in its first year, so the wine), the Rabbis fit — the parameter wine_age on F3 master_row.
- Menachot 87a:24 [LINK — Num 28:29] — DISPUTE. R. Meir: 'a tenth, a tenth' (28:29) = TWO measuring vessels (heaped and level) — the distributive read as two vessels; F4's tenth (the parameter tenth_vessels).
- Menachot 87b:2 [LINK — Num 28:29 Num 29:4] — DISPUTE. the Rabbis: ONE tenth-vessel ('ONE tenth-part', 29:4) and the repetition a half-tenth vessel — the parameter's other arm.
- Menachot 87b:5 [LINK — Num 29:15] — LAW. THE DOT on the vav of 'a tenth' at 29:15 (the first day of Sukkot — the puncta) — R. Yosei: measure not with a three-tenth or two-tenth vessel; R. Meir expounds no dot — an INK MARK read as law: F6 the_multiplied_table's dotted-tenth row (the mark checked on the DB).
- Menachot 89a:15 [LINK — Num 28:14] — LAW. six, four, three LOG from 28:14's half, third and quarter of the hin — F3 master_row = SH.LOGS by CALL (the hin twelve log).
- Menachot 91b:12 [LINK — Num 28:12 — CREDITED: num_13_15_shelach_exam_2026-09-10.md] — DERIVATION. CREDITED (the Shelach exam) — the ram's two tenths at 28:12 beside 15:6: the redundancy includes AARON'S RAM of Yom Kippur in the libations — the Shelach runner's cell.
- Menachot 104a:11 [LINK — Num 28:14 — CREDITED: num_13_15_shelach_exam_2026-09-10.md] — LAW. CREDITED (the Shelach exam) — 'SHALL BE' (28:14) admits independent libations beyond the table; not less than three log by 15:13 — the Shelach runner's row libation_floors READ by CALL.
- Menachot 104b:8 [LINK — Num 29:39] — LAW. a burnt offering brought IN PARTNERSHIP — 'YOUR burnt offerings' plural (29:39); a meal offering not (Lev 2:1 'a soul') — F7 these_you_shall_do's partnership row (olah yes, minchah no).
- Menachot 107a:12 [LINK — Num 28:14 — CREDITED: num_13_15_shelach_exam_2026-09-10.md] — LAW. CREDITED (the Shelach exam) — the same 'shall be' (28:14) and 15:13's floor — the Shelach runner's libation_floors.

## The finds (this docket's crowns)
- R. AKIVA'S ARGUMENT IS THE PARSER'S TWO ORDERS (Menachot 45b:10, 45b:13): are Leviticus's Shavuot sheep Numbers'? "when you reach the bulls and rams" — ONE bull and TWO rams (Lev 23:18) against TWO bulls and ONE ram (28:27), and the ORDER differs — Leviticus writes sheep, bull, rams; Numbers bulls, ram, sheep: the reading's [7, 1, 2] against [2, 1, 7] argued on the Talmud's page; R. Shimon: NUMBERS' SET RAN IN THE WILDERNESS, Leviticus's not until the land (45b:6, 45b:11 — the tape's own claim); Chagigah 6a:9 counts LEVITICUS's set ("one bull, two rams, seven sheep") for Beit Shammai's value argument — the answer sheet picks a set per question.
- THE STACK'S ARITHMETIC ON FOUR PAGES: twenty-two lambs on a Sukkot Sabbath = 6 + 2 + 7 + 7 (Menachot 49b:5); SIXTEEN lambs on the first of Tishri = the tamid's 2 + Rosh Hashanah's 7 + the new moon's 7 (Arakhin 13a:10 — 29:6's "besides the burnt offering of the month" computed); 70 = 24 × 2 + 22 over the watches (Sukkah 55b:8) and the watches' division a function of the declining table, the seventh day all equal 7 + 2 + 1 + 14 = 24 (Sukkah 55b:1-4); the six-lamb floor = three days' temidim (Arakhin 13a:9-11); Hillel's "MORE THAN TWO HUNDRED" Sabbath-overriding offerings a year (Pesachim 66a:3) — every one a checked row for the runner.
- THE WATER LIBATION'S FOUR SOURCES, ALL ON THE SHELF: R. Akiva's induction from the seasons "that the year's rains be blessed" (Rosh Hashanah 16a:15); ben Beteira's THREE LETTERS mem, yod, mem across "their libationS" (29:19), "itS libations" (29:31), "their ordinance" (29:33) = water (Taanit 2b:14; the day-counts each source yields computed at 3a:3), licensed by Rav Chisda's "a closed letter rendered open is valid" (Shabbat 103b:12, 103b:16); R. Nathan's doubled libation-root at 28:7 with its varied prefixes = one of water, one of wine (Taanit 3a:2); and A HALAKHAH TO MOSES FROM SINAI (the received arm — Taanit 3a:6-7, Sukkah 34a:2, 44a:5, Zevachim 110b:6) — the parameter water_libation_source carries four arms, the data channel self-labeled on the fourth.
- THE VAV OF "AND A GOAT" (Shevuot 9b:2, 9b:12, 10a:8): R. Chama b. R. Chanina — the festivals' goat lines read "AND a goat" where "a goat" would do, the conjunction juxtaposing each to the NEW MOON'S goat at the passage's head; and Shavuot's and Yom Kippur's goats carry NO vav (10a:11) — the thirteen goat lines' vav census is a token fact the runner reads off the ink; R. Yochanan's bar: in consecrated matters no juxtaposition is derived from a juxtaposition (10a:9) — every goat runs DIRECTLY to the first.
- "TO THE LORD" AT THE NEW MOON'S GOAT ALONE (Shevuot 9a:7-9): the register's count (one "for a sin offering to the LORD" beside eleven bare) is the Talmud's question — Reish Lakish's "an atonement for My diminishing the moon" and "a sin the LORD alone knows" BOTH from the one preposition ("of the LORD" would serve one, "for the LORD" the other — the lamed yields two); the scope by the FIXED-TIME analogy to Yom Kippur's goat (9a:10) — the period timers' own property the middah's ground.
- THREE TANNAIM ON THE THIRTEEN GOATS (Mishnah Shevuot 1:4-5 at 2a:8-2b:7; 10a:14-16): R. Yehuda one job (the Temple's defiling unknown at both ends), R. Shimon three tiers (new moon / festivals / Yom Kippur's outer goat), R. Meir flat with the INNER goat excepted; THE INTERCHANGE RING — a festival's goat not offered goes to a new moon, then to Yom Kippur, then to a festival (Shevuot 12b:4; consecration fixes the genus, the knife the species, 12b:3).
- NUM 29:1 "A DAY OF TERUAH" IN THE SHOFAR'S COUNT (Rosh Hashanah 34a:10-12): three teruot for Rosh Hashanah — Lev 23:24 and Lev 25:9 by the Torah, and OUR VERSE "comes for its own statement" — the peg of the wilderness analogy (34a:8, the Beha runner's "terua"/"terua") and "a DAY of teruah": BY DAY, NOT AT NIGHT — the blowing's slot from the verse's own word; the INSTRUMENT is not in the verse — the shofar from the Jubilee's "seventh month" (33b:11) and from the covered moon of Ps 81:4, the one festival at a new moon (34a:19); Abaye: "and we TRANSLATE it a day of yevava (wailing)" — Onkelos cited by name on 29:1 (33b:10), the reading's own crown.
- THE SINAI OLAH IS THE TAMID — BY ONE HOUSE (Chagigah 6a:15-16): Beit Hillel, R. Akiva, R. Yosei HaGelili — the burnt offering of Exod 24:5 was the DAILY offering (28:6's "made at Mount Sinai"); Beit Shammai, R. Elazar (R. Yishmael deleted from the list, 6b:11) — an appearance olah; R. ELAZAR ON 28:6: "its details said at Sinai, itself not offered until the Tabernacle stood" (6b:3) — THE TAPE'S OWN TIMER (the tamid's debt opened at Exod 40:29, the erection) is his arm; R. Akiva's "never ceased" against Amos 5:25's forty years (6b:4-5).
- THE MUSAF AS A SANCTITY MARKER (Chagigah 18a:6): the a fortiori for the intermediate days refuted by the six weekdays (NO musaf), re-refuted by the NEW MOON (a musaf, labor permitted — the register's own count: no rest token at 28:11-15), settled by "a holy convocation" — the tradition grades days by our tables' presence.
- THE COUNT'S UNIT IS THE SANCTIFICATION'S SPAN (Rosh Hashanah 5a:4; Chagigah 17b:7): count thirty days and sanctify the month with offerings, count from Pesach and sanctify Shavuot with offerings — the month's unit a day, Shavuot's a week: SEVEN DAYS OF REDRESS (Chagigah 17a:5, Shavuot likened to Pesach by Deut 16:16) with the DAY OF SLAUGHTER after a Friday Shavuot (Mishnah Chagigah 2:4 at 17a:3); "grasped many, grasped nothing" the tie-breaker (Rosh Hashanah 4b:15; Chagigah 17a:8).
- THE EIGHTH DAY'S TWO ANSWERS ON ONE PAGE (Rosh Hashanah 4b:13; Chagigah 17a:6-7; Beitzah 20b:5-6): a festival of its own for the six marks (the lottery, the time-blessing, the festival's name, the offering, the song, the blessing — PZR KShB, Sukkah 48a:1) and SUKKOT'S for the redress; Rav Pappa's proof from the table — ONE bull where the decline would give six (Sukkah 47a:8); the head WITHOUT the conjunction, "on the eighth day" against "and on the second day" (47a:9 — a token fact to check at 29:35 against 29:17-32); the STAY after the festival by Pesach's "you shall turn in the morning" (Chagigah 17a:9, 17b:1; Pesachim 95b:6-8).
- "ITS DAY PASSED, ITS SACRIFICE IS INVALID" (Berakhot 26a:17): prayer in place of sacrifice would forbid the make-up — the offering's due CANNOT be paid late: the tamid_owed debit lapses at the day's end, the timer's miss a miss; the additional prayer's window all day or until seven hours (Mishnah Berakhot 4:1 at 26a:11-12) — the musaf's hour read into the prayer.
- THE SUPPLY MODEL (Shevuot 10b:7, 11a:8, 11b:11, 12a:7, 12b:3): the surplus tamid lambs redeemed unblemished by the court's tacit stipulation (the Rabbis) or offered as the altar's "dessert" (R. Shimon); communal consecration fixes the GENUS, the knife the species — the tables' animals one stock named at slaughter; the six-lamb chamber (Mishnah Arakhin 2:5).
- THE FESTIVAL'S LICENSED SLAUGHTER (Mishnah Beitzah 2:4 at 19a:11; 20b:4): Beit Shammai permit no burnt offering on a festival "apart from the daily and additional offerings of the day"; Beit Hillel's a fortiori runs FROM the Sabbath's musaf ("where the commoner's slaughter is barred, the Most High's is permitted") — 28:9-10 the premise both houses hold; "your table full and your Master's table empty" (Abba Shaul, 20b:8) — the altar's bread of 28:2.
- THE INK'S SMALL MARKS ON THE SHELF: THE DOTTED TENTH at 29:15 (Menachot 87b:5 — the puncta a row to check on the store); the vav of 29:4's "and a tenth" expounded or not (87b:3); the hin twelve log by the numerical value of "THIS" (Menachot 89a:16) and R. Elazar b. Azarya's "a halakhah to Moses from Sinai" for the log measures (89a:7 — the data channel self-labeled); the five afflictions from five mentions, 29:7 among them (Yoma 76a:11); SHEKHAR's three senses — drinking, satiation, intoxication (Sukkah 49b:5).
- THE RUN IN THE WRITINGS AND THE PROPHETS: 2 Chronicles 2:3 — Solomon's four tiers "morning and evening, the Sabbaths, the new moons, the festivals" (Menachot 110a:9); 2 Chronicles 29:27 the song with the burnt offering and 5:13 the trumpets over THE TEMIDIM AND THE MUSAFIM (Sukkah 51a:12-13); EZEKIEL 46:6-7's altered new-moon table read as the AVAILABILITY LADDER — two bulls found not, one; seven lambs not, six, down to one (Menachot 45a:6-8) — and Chanina b. Chizkiya's reconciliation charter for Ezekiel (45a:19); Amos 5:25 and Ps 81:4 above.
- THE IMPURITY OF THE DEEP (Pesachim 80b:5; 81a:14-15; 81b:3): the Sifrei 145:3's term at the new moon's goat is the answer sheet's clause for the Pesach — the concealed grave the frontplate appeases; Rava reads "its appointed time" of 28:2 with 9:13 to extend it to the tamid, Rabba's a fortiori from the communal override.
- THE VOW DEADLINE (Beitzah 19b:8-13): "you shall not delay" measured in festivals — three in any order (the first tanna), three in their order with Sukkot last (R. Shimon), by Sukkot whenever vowed (R. Elazar b. R. Shimon) — the calendar's timers the deadline's clock; the two loaves' baking overrides neither Shabbat nor the festival (20b:11); Shavuot's lambs at the wrong time valid, the blood not sprinkled on Shabbat when it serves nothing (20b:14).
- RULES ABOUT RULES, LOGGED FOR MIDDOT: a verbal analogy prefers the IDENTICAL FORM — "they shall be" from "they shall be", not from a differing form (Menachot 45b:18-20); ONE AMPLIFICATION AFTER ANOTHER RESTRICTS (89a:3); the METHOD FORK named as a tannaitic dispute — generalizations-and-details against amplifications-and-restrictions (Sukkah 50b:5); juxtaposition yields to the verbal analogy when both stand (Rosh Hashanah 34a:5); no juxtaposition from a juxtaposition in consecrated matters (Shevuot 10a:9); an individual's offering not from the communal, the perpetual not from the one-time (Chagigah 6a:11, 6a:13); the lamed's double yield (Shevuot 9a:9).

## Corrections (appended at the runner step, 2026-09-11 — the ledger is append-only)
- The crown "THE STACK'S ARITHMETIC ON FOUR PAGES" typed "twenty-two lambs on a Sukkot Sabbath = 6 + 2 + 7 + 7 (Menachot 49b:5)" from memory; the page's own composition (read again when the runner's first typed stack came out 20) is THREE DAYS — Shabbat and the two days of Rosh Hashanah: the temidim 2 x 3, the Sabbath's musaf 2, Rosh Hashanah's 7, the new moon's 7 = 22. The row Menachot 49b:5 above has it right; the runner computes it so (LAMBS_THREE_DAYS).
